UNCOMMITTED on disk, awaiting "save".** Done via systematic-debugging → TDD (superpowers). **Root cause:** `find_verse_region_b` ended verse N's region at the next verse's `<span class="vn">`, but that span sits INSIDE its `<a class="vn-link">` open tag — so the region carried the next anchor's open tag at its tail, and a verse-end (empty-anchor) marker (topical/dict notes) landed between that `<a>` and its `<span>` = `<a>` in `<a>` (the asymmetry with Strategy A, which ends at the next vn-link's `<`). Confirmed by a 2-verse repro reproducing the exact `_BROKEN_RE` shape before fixing. **Fix:** back the region end up past an immediately-preceding `<a class="vn-link" …>` open tag (mirrors Strategy A); bare-span fallback unchanged; the spill resolver inherits it. **TDD** (`tests/test_inject_wellformed.py` +2): a region-boundary test + an end-to-end "verse-level inject → 0 nested `<a>`" test, both RED→GREEN. **NO re-bake** (committed base already `--fix`'d to 0; inject `already_in` = no-op; bare-base regen is lossy) — the fix is preventive, so `check_nested_anchors --fix` is now a safety net, not a per-ingest necessity. **GATES:** 2 new + `test_inject_wellformed` 21 + `test_nested_anchors` (base-zero gate) + spill/irregular 6 + marker/resync 42 green · `check_nested_anchors` (no `--fix`) **0 across 61 files** (base untouched) · `lint_rules` 16/0/0 · `ruff format --check` clean. NEW `scripts/api/covers.py::api_apply_cover_template(edition_id, stem)` (audit-logged) — validate stem∈COVER_TEMPLATES + edition → `generate_edition_covers._compose_cover(stem, title_for_edition(id))` → `ensure_backup` + `atomic_write_bytes` to `covers/<id>.jpg` → `api_save_edition_meta({cover_image, cover_template})`; routed `POST /api/covers/<ed>/template` in `_POST_ROUTES` (distinct suffix from the `/main` multipart + DELETE routes). `/customize` "Cover" panel (static markup in `renderEditions`, behaviour in NEW `wireCoversSection` — no innerHTML reassignment, security-hook-clean): clickable 25-thumbnail picker (thumbs = sandbox-served `/content/covers/templates/<stem>.png`; pick → immediate server recompose → in-place preview refresh) + main-cover (`/main`) & per-book-art (`/book/<code>`) upload affordances. **23 tests** (`tests/test_cover_templates.py`). **Verified LIVE in-browser** (Playwright): 11 cover sections × 25 thumbs render, real click → recompose → "Cover updated." + preview refresh; all editions interactive (see fix #1). **TITLE-OVERFLOW FIX (user-reported):** `_compose_cover` drew at a fixed 72pt with no width handling → long titles ran into the border art. NEW `_fit_title_font` auto-shrinks 72→28pt (4pt steps) until the widest line fits a **150px side safe-zone**; the 4 long-title covers (ethiopian/catholic/anglican/lutheran) regenerated at 64pt with ≥162px side clearance, the other 5 unchanged at 72pt. **3 PRE-EXISTING BUGS FIXED (surfaced by live browser QA):** (1) **`/customize` wired ONLY the first edition** — the rebind loop's `wrap.querySelectorAll('[data-edition]')` ALSO matches each edition's psi11 Preview button; on the first such box `btn` is null and the unguarded `btn.addEventListener` threw (swallowed by a try/catch) → the `forEach` aborted → editions 2-11 had NO handlers at all (save · popup-langs · traditions · reading-plans · covers). Fix: `if (!btn) return;` guard + regression test `test_edition_rebind_loop_skips_non_card_boxes`. **Had been broken for 10/11 editions; source-string tests couldn't catch it.** (2) **SQLite cross-thread 500** — `/customize` + `/matrix` 500'd in the RUNNING app: `corpus_index.connection()` cached a conn opened in the `_warm_corpus_index` (main) thread, reused on `ThreadingHTTPServer` request threads. Fix: `sqlite3.connect(..., check_same_thread=False)` (index is read-only-by-convention; writes use a separate conn in `rebuild()`) + cross-thread regression test. (3) **5 cover-template PNGs were actually JPEGs misnamed `.png`** (the `*_red` variants) → `_send_file`'s SEC-001 magic-byte guard 415'd them (broken picker thumbnails). Re-encoded to real PNG (lossless) + guard test `test_every_template_file_is_really_a_png`. Also repaired a stale pre-existing test (`querySelectorAll('input, select')` → `…, textarea')`, stale since Phase 1 added the description/dedication textareas). **GATES:** 221 tests green · `lint_rules` 16/0/0 · ruff clean · editions.yaml unchanged. **⚠ Could NOT capture a confirming screenshot — the Playwright session repeatedly closed mid-run; title-fit is proven by test + measurement (all 9 titles ≤724px after fit) — USER to eyeball the 4 regenerated covers + flag any that still crowd.** **▶ NEXT — Wave 3 (EPUB Presentation Phase 2; the BASE RE-BAKE wave, riskiest + most Claude-dependent):** the remaining 3 enum settings (`marker_style`→numbers · `verse_popup_style` · `note_popup_style`) · symbols-into-notes (+ symbol→glossary link) · widened/drop-KJV popups · Nave's topical back-matter page.
